<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://deepmind.google/models/gemini/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Gemini 4 Argon</span><span class='zh'>Gemini 4 Argon 模型</span></a><span class="dom">deepmind.google</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Gemini 4 Argon is the latest in Google&#x27;s Gemini lineup, offering stronger reasoning, higher factual accuracy, and more reliable tool usage across tasks.</span><span class='zh'>Gemini 4 Argon 是谷歌 Gemini 系列的最新版本，提升了推理能力、事实准确性和跨任务的工具使用稳定性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Gemini 4 Argon is the latest in Google&#x27;s Gemini lineup, offering stronger reasoning, higher factual accuracy, and more reliable tool usage across tasks. It signals continued maturation of high-end multilingual agents aimed at developers and enterprises, with an emphasis on safety, efficiency, and real-world usefulness.</span><span class='zh'>Gemini 4 Argon 是谷歌 Gemini 系列的最新版本，提升了推理能力、事实准确性和跨任务的工具使用稳定性。该发布体现高端多语言代理的持续成熟，重点放在安全性、效率与实际应用价值。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Apple announces HomeView, a new feature aimed at simplifying how users view and control smart devices in the home.</span><span class='zh'>苹果宣布了 HomeView，旨在简化家庭中智能设备的查看与控制。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Apple announces HomeView, a new feature aimed at simplifying how users view and control smart devices in the home. It underscores Apple&#x27;s continuing effort to deepen the Home ecosystem with privacy-conscious design and tighter device interoperability.</span><span class='zh'>苹果宣布了 HomeView，旨在简化家庭中智能设备的查看与控制。此举体现苹果继续扩展家居生态的决心，强调隐私设计与设备互操作性的提升。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.firefox.com/en-US/enterprise/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Firefox Enterprise</span><span class='zh'>Mozilla 推出 Firefox 企业版</span></a><span class="dom">firefox.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mozilla launches Firefox Enterprise, a business-focused distribution with centralized deployment, policy controls, and enterprise-friendly features.</span><span class='zh'>Mozilla 推出面向企业的 Firefox Enterprise，提供集中部署、策略控制和面向企业的安全特性，便于 IT 管理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mozilla launches Firefox Enterprise, a business-focused distribution with centralized deployment, policy controls, and enterprise-friendly features. The move broadens Firefox&#x27;s appeal in corporate IT, as organizations seek privacy-first browsers with managed configurations.</span><span class='zh'>Mozilla 推出面向企业的 Firefox Enterprise，提供集中部署、策略控制和面向企业的安全特性，便于 IT 管理。此举有助于 Firefox 在企业环境中的竞争力，满足组织对隐私与可控配置的需求。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/rust-lang/rust/issues/164250" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">04</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rust-lang/rust/issues/164250" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>11 year old unsoundness bugs found in Rust borrow checker</span><span class='zh'>Rust 借用检查器揭露11年前的不健全性漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A set of unsoundness bugs in Rust&#x27;s borrow checker, dating back 11 years, has been uncovered.</span><span class='zh'>揭示了 Rust 的借用检查器中存在的11年前的不健全性漏洞，凸显在复杂类型系统中长期保持内存安全的挑战。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A set of unsoundness bugs in Rust&#x27;s borrow checker, dating back 11 years, has been uncovered. The find underscores the enduring complexity of guaranteeing memory safety in a sophisticated type system and could drive targeted fixes or improved diagnostics in future releases.</span><span class='zh'>揭示了 Rust 的借用检查器中存在的11年前的不健全性漏洞，凸显在复杂类型系统中长期保持内存安全的挑战。此发现可能推动针对性修复与改进诊断能力，影响未来版本的安全性保障。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.thunderbird.net/2026/10/thunderbird-for-ios-a-first-look-at-the-native-iphone-email-app-in-development/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Thunderbird for iOS</span><span class='zh'>Thunderbird iOS 版</span></a><span class="dom">blog.thunderbird.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Thunderbird is sharing a first look at a native iOS email app in development, signaling a major milestone for the open-source project.</span><span class='zh'>Thunderbird 正在展示其原生 iOS 邮件应用的初步样子，标志着这家开源项目的一大里程碑。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Thunderbird is sharing a first look at a native iOS email app in development, signaling a major milestone for the open-source project. The iOS app aims to deliver a privacy-conscious, performant experience in the Apple ecosystem while staying aligned with Thunderbird&#x27;s cross-platform ethos.</span><span class='zh'>Thunderbird 正在展示其原生 iOS 邮件应用的初步样子，标志着这家开源项目的一大里程碑。iOS 版本计划在 Apple 生态中提供注重隐私、性能出色的邮件体验，同时坚持 Thunderbird 的跨平台理念。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Regulatory staff were instructed to retract analysis of suspicious oil trades, suggesting external pressure influencing enforcement work.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，美国商品期货交易委员会的工作人员被指示撤回对可疑石油交易的分析，显示监管工作可能受到外部压力的影响。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Regulatory staff were instructed to retract analysis of suspicious oil trades, suggesting external pressure influencing enforcement work. The episode highlights concerns about independence and transparency in market surveillance and regulatory credibility.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，美国商品期货交易委员会的工作人员被指示撤回对可疑石油交易的分析，显示监管工作可能受到外部压力的影响。此事引发对执法独立性、透明度与市场监管可信度的担忧。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">07</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://eg2.haku.sh/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>We turned EmbeddingGemma 2 into a speech-to-text model</span><span class='zh'>EmbeddingGemma 2 已改造成语音转文本模型</span></a><span class="dom">eg2.haku.sh</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>EmbeddingGemma 2 is repurposed as a speech-to-text model, demonstrating that a robust embedding backbone can be adapted for ASR without building from scratch.</span><span class='zh'>EmbeddingGemma 2 被改造为语音转文本模型，展示了以强大嵌入骨干为基础也能用于语音识别，而无需从头构建声学模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>EmbeddingGemma 2 is repurposed as a speech-to-text model, demonstrating that a robust embedding backbone can be adapted for ASR without building from scratch. The approach highlights the flexibility of embedding-based architectures and could offer cost-effective, multilingual transcription options in practice.</span><span class='zh'>EmbeddingGemma 2 被改造为语音转文本模型，展示了以强大嵌入骨干为基础也能用于语音识别，而无需从头构建声学模型。该方法凸显了嵌入式架构的灵活性，并有望带来更具成本效益的多语言转写方案。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.thehistoryblog.com/archives/77107" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Hittite royal archive found in Turkey</span><span class='zh'>土耳其发现的赫梯王室档案</span></a><span class="dom">thehistoryblog.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Archaeologists uncovered a royal archive from the Hittite civilization in Turkey, revealing tablets and inscriptions that illuminate ancient governance, diplomacy, and daily life.</span><span class='zh'>考古学家在土耳其发现了赫梯王朝的皇家档案，出土的泥板与铭文有助于揭示当时的统治、外交与日常生活。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Archaeologists uncovered a royal archive from the Hittite civilization in Turkey, revealing tablets and inscriptions that illuminate ancient governance, diplomacy, and daily life. The find could reshape understanding of Bronze Age Anatolia and offer new data for linguistics and history.</span><span class='zh'>考古学家在土耳其发现了赫梯王朝的皇家档案，出土的泥板与铭文有助于揭示当时的统治、外交与日常生活。此发现可能重塑对安纳托利亚青铜时代的认识，并为语言学和历史学提供新材料。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://yyhh.org/blog/2026/10/ai-is-a-tool/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI is a tool and using it well takes skill</span><span class='zh'>AI 是工具，善用它需要技巧</span></a><span class="dom">yyhh.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that AI is a tool and its value comes from how skillfully it is used, urging engineers to develop practical workflows, critical thinking, and governance.</span><span class='zh'>文章将 AI 视为工具，强调其价值取决于使用者的技能水平，呼吁建立实用工作流、批判性思维和治理机制以避免滥用与过度乐观。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that AI is a tool and its value comes from how skillfully it is used, urging engineers to develop practical workflows, critical thinking, and governance.</span><span class='zh'>文章将 AI 视为工具，强调其价值取决于使用者的技能水平，呼吁建立实用工作流、批判性思维和治理机制以避免滥用与过度乐观。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.character.ai/an-update-on-character-ai/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Character.ai has initiated voluntary Chapter 11 bankruptcy</span><span class='zh'>Character.ai 已自愿进入第十一章破产保护</span></a><span class="dom">blog.character.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Character.ai has voluntarily filed for Chapter 11 bankruptcy to restructure its debt while continuing operations.</span><span class='zh'>Character.ai 自愿申请第十一章破产保护以进行债务重组，同时继续运营。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Character.ai has voluntarily filed for Chapter 11 bankruptcy to restructure its debt while continuing operations. The move aims to stabilize the business and preserve user access, though it raises questions about platform uptime and long-term AI feature plans.</span><span class='zh'>Character.ai 自愿申请第十一章破产保护以进行债务重组，同时继续运营。此举意在稳定业务、维持用户访问，但也让人关注平台的持续运行与长期 AI 功能计划。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.tomshardware.com/tech-industry/cyber-security/unprecedented-cyberattack-cripples-supermarket-fridges-across-europe-as-hackers-activate-automatic-defrosting-delivery-and-store-prices-also-impacted-by-incident" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Hackers activate automatic defrosting in supermarket fridges across Europe</span><span class='zh'>黑客在欧洲多国超市冷藏设备上启用自动除霜</span></a><span class="dom">tomshardware.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A coordinated cyberattack triggered automatic defrosting across supermarket fridges in Europe, disrupting storage conditions and potentially impacting pricing and stock.</span><span class='zh'>一次跨国网络攻击促使欧洲多家超市的冷藏设备启用自动除霜，干扰了冷藏条件，可能影响库存与价格。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A coordinated cyberattack triggered automatic defrosting across supermarket fridges in Europe, disrupting storage conditions and potentially impacting pricing and stock. The incident underscores how IoT-enabled retail devices can be manipulated at scale, highlighting security gaps in supply chains and the importance of robust device hygiene.</span><span class='zh'>一次跨国网络攻击促使欧洲多家超市的冷藏设备启用自动除霜，干扰了冷藏条件，可能影响库存与价格。这一事件凸显了零售物联网设备的安全脆弱性，以及对供应链安全治理的迫切需求。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>871</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50055304' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>971</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50060694" data-id="50060694" data-tags="Programming|Security|Code" data-score="592" data-comments="288" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50060694" data-time="1791829744">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/openai/codex/issues/48195" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">13</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openai/codex/issues/48195" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Codex CLI installs a persistent, self-updating background daemon by default</span><span class='zh'>Codex CLI 默认安装一个持久且自更新的后台守护进程</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Codex CLI now ships with a background daemon that persists and self-updates by default, raising concerns about user consent, security, and potential persistence.</span><span class='zh'>Codex CLI 现默认随附一个持续运行并自更新的后台守护进程，引发对用户同意、安全性及长期持久化的担忧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Codex CLI now ships with a background daemon that persists and self-updates by default, raising concerns about user consent, security, and potential persistence. Critics warn this can create a form of &#x27;auto-update&#x27; risk and complicate auditing. This change could influence how developers package CLIs and how users trust automation.</span><span class='zh'>Codex CLI 现默认随附一个持续运行并自更新的后台守护进程，引发对用户同意、安全性及长期持久化的担忧。有人担心这类自动更新会带来可追溯性和审计难题。这一变动可能影响开发者在打包 CLI 时的做法以及用户对自动化工具的信任。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>592</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50060694' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>288</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50058528" data-id="50058528" data-tags="Programming|Web" data-score="680" data-comments="303" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50058528" data-time="1791821590">
<a class="thumb" href="https://prophitt.me/writing/a-modern-web-app-in-c" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=prophitt.me&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>prophitt.me</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">14</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://prophitt.me/writing/a-modern-web-app-in-c" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A Modern Web App in C</span><span class='zh'>用 C 构建现代 Web 应用</span></a><span class="dom">prophitt.me</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The article argues that modern web apps can be built in C, likely via WebAssembly, highlighting architecture, tooling, and performance trade-offs.</span><span class='zh'>本文探讨如何用 C 构建现代 Web 应用，可能通过 WebAssembly 实现前端与后端的高性能协作，分析架构、工具链与性能取舍。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The article argues that modern web apps can be built in C, likely via WebAssembly, highlighting architecture, tooling, and performance trade-offs. It challenges conventional language choices and invites reconsideration of full-stack approach.</span><span class='zh'>本文探讨如何用 C 构建现代 Web 应用，可能通过 WebAssembly 实现前端与后端的高性能协作，分析架构、工具链与性能取舍。它挑战了传统的语言选择观，鼓励重新考虑全栈开发的路径。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://z.ai/blog/glm-legal" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GLM-Legal</span><span class='zh'>GLM 法律问题</span></a><span class="dom">z.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The post outlines legal considerations for GLMs, including licensing, data rights, and compliance.</span><span class='zh'>本文概述了 GLM 的法律考量，包括许可、数据权利和合规性，讨论了在部署 GLM 时开发者和组织应如何进行风险管理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The post outlines legal considerations for GLMs, including licensing, data rights, and compliance. It discusses risk management for developers and organizations deploying GLMs.</span><span class='zh'>本文概述了 GLM 的法律考量，包括许可、数据权利和合规性，讨论了在部署 GLM 时开发者和组织应如何进行风险管理。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13928" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI models can provably hide arbitrary capabilities</span><span class='zh'>AI 模型可被证明隐藏任意能力</span></a><span class="dom">arxiv.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers show that AI models can provably conceal arbitrary capabilities, undermining safety evaluations that rely on probing for dangerous behaviors.</span><span class='zh'>研究表明，AI 模型在理论上可以证明性地隐藏任意能力，从而削弱基于探测的安全评估。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers show that AI models can provably conceal arbitrary capabilities, undermining safety evaluations that rely on probing for dangerous behaviors. The result formalizes a worst‑case scenario where a model can hide malicious or sensitive behaviors unless comprehensive testing covers all prompts and contexts. The finding underscores the need for robust evaluation and red‑teaming methods beyond surface‑level checks.</span><span class='zh'>研究表明，AI 模型在理论上可以证明性地隐藏任意能力，从而削弱基于探测的安全评估。研究给出一个最坏情形的框架：若测试覆盖的提示和情境不全，模型就可能隐藏恶意或敏感行为。此发现强调需要比表层测试更健全的评估与红队方法。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.argmin.net/p/faster-fourier-transforms" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A look at the math that OpenAI claims computes faster Fourier transforms</span><span class='zh'>看 OpenAI 声称加速傅里叶变换的数学原理</span></a><span class="dom">argmin.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI reportedly has a mathematical approach that can compute Fourier transforms faster than conventional FFTs.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 提出一种据称能更快计算傅里叶变换的数学方法，潜在地降低大规模 AI 与信号处理任务的计算成本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI reportedly has a mathematical approach that can compute Fourier transforms faster than conventional FFTs. The idea could reduce compute for large-scale AI tasks and signal-processing workloads, but its value will depend on numerical stability and practical implementation. If proven robust, it could influence future libraries and hardware optimizations.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 提出一种据称能更快计算傅里叶变换的数学方法，潜在地降低大规模 AI 与信号处理任务的计算成本。效果取决于数值稳定性与实际实现的可行性。若证实稳健，可能推动相关库和硬件优化的改进。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://nonfunctional.substack.com/p/passkeys-are-a-trap-do-not-use-them" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Passkeys are a trap</span><span class='zh'>Passkeys 是陷阱。请不要使用它们。</span></a><span class="dom">nonfunctional.substack.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A provocative stance arguing passkeys introduce risk, friction, or vendor lock-in rather than solving security problems.</span><span class='zh'>这篇观点认为 Passkeys 并未真正解决安全问题，反而带来新的风险、使用摩擦和厂商锁定。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A provocative stance arguing passkeys introduce risk, friction, or vendor lock-in rather than solving security problems. It cautions readers to consider trade-offs and to explore alternatives like hardware-backed keys or password managers. The piece invites debate about modern authentication design.</span><span class='zh'>这篇观点认为 Passkeys 并未真正解决安全问题，反而带来新的风险、使用摩擦和厂商锁定。强调权衡取舍并探讨硬件密钥、密码管理工具等替代方案，鼓励对现代认证设计的讨论。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI coding agents can generate more code, but this doesn&#x27;t translate into more usable software due to quality and integration hurdles.</span><span class='zh'>AI 编码代理确实能产生更多代码，但由于质量、模块化和集成方面的挑战，未能带来更多可用软件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI coding agents can generate more code, but this doesn&#x27;t translate into more usable software due to quality and integration hurdles. The article underscores the need for better tooling and governance in AI-assisted development.</span><span class='zh'>AI 编码代理确实能产生更多代码，但由于质量、模块化和集成方面的挑战，未能带来更多可用软件。此现象强调在 AI 辅助开发中需要更好的工具链、标准与治理。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.spinellis.gr/blog/20261008/?li261008" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Thinking will become a hobby</span><span class='zh'>思考将成为一种爱好</span></a><span class="dom">spinellis.gr</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>With AI taking over much routine reasoning, thinking might become a hobby rather than everyday work, the post argues.</span><span class='zh'>随着 AI 接管大量常规推理，思考可能从日常工作转变为一种爱好。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>With AI taking over much routine reasoning, thinking might become a hobby rather than everyday work, the post argues. It encourages deliberate practice of critical thinking, problem framing, and creative exploration to stay valuable.</span><span class='zh'>随着 AI 接管大量常规推理，思考可能从日常工作转变为一种爱好。文章鼓励有意识地练习批判性思维、问题框定与创造性探索，以保持自身价值。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Describes how Firefox enables resumable downloads for large files, including range requests, server support, and resilience against network interruptions. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.linkedin.com/pulse/ai-tool-rich-hickey-kjl8e" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>&#x27;AI&#x27; Is Not a Tool by Rich Hickey</span><span class='zh'>Rich Hickey 的《AI 不是工具》</span></a><span class="dom">linkedin.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Rich Hickey challenges the common framing of AI as a mere tool, arguing that treating AI as just another utility misses deeper shifts in software design.</span><span class='zh'>Rich Hickey 质疑将 AI 简化为单纯工具的观点，认为这忽视了软件设计领域的深层变革。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Rich Hickey challenges the common framing of AI as a mere tool, arguing that treating AI as just another utility misses deeper shifts in software design. He advocates new mental models for data, computation, and governance to build reliable, long-lasting AI systems.</span><span class='zh'>Rich Hickey 质疑将 AI 简化为单纯工具的观点，认为这忽视了软件设计领域的深层变革。他主张在数据、计算与治理层面建立新的思维模型，以构建更可靠、可持续的 AI 系统。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c620r33plgppo" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Citroen revives the 2CV for an electric age</span><span class='zh'>Citroën 将 2CV 在电动时代重新诠释</span></a><span class="dom">bbc.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Citroën revives the 2CV design with an electric powertrain, blending retro styling with modern EV tech.</span><span class='zh'>Citroën 将 2CV 的经典设计与电动动力系统相结合，展现了在可持续性背景下对传统车型的再造。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Citroën revives the 2CV design with an electric powertrain, blending retro styling with modern EV tech. The move showcases how nostalgic models can be reimagined for sustainability, while presenting challenges in preserving iconic character and efficiency.</span><span class='zh'>Citroën 将 2CV 的经典设计与电动动力系统相结合，展现了在可持续性背景下对传统车型的再造。此举既保留了怀旧风格，也带来在保持标志性特征与效率之间的平衡挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/prabanta-dev/janas" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Janas: Run LLMs larger than RAM by streaming experts from SSD</span><span class='zh'>Janas：通过从 SSD 流式加载专家参数，在 RAM 之外运行超大规模 LLM</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Janas enables running LLMs larger than available RAM by streaming model components from SSD as needed.</span><span class='zh'>Janas 通过按需从 SSD 流式加载模型部分来在内存超限的情况下运行超大规模的 LLM。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Janas enables running LLMs larger than available RAM by streaming model components from SSD as needed. By using a mixture-of-experts design, it fetches parts of the model only when required, reducing memory pressure without needing massive hardware upgrades. The approach could lower the bar for experimenting with large models on commodity machines.</span><span class='zh'>Janas 通过按需从 SSD 流式加载模型部分来在内存超限的情况下运行超大规模的 LLM。它利用专家模型的分布式架构，只有在需要时才获取相应参数，降低对内存的压力，无需巨额硬件升级。该思路若规模化，将降低在普通硬件上尝试大模型的门槛。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>211</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50063717' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>81</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50063831" data-id="50063831" data-tags="Entertainment" data-score="207" data-comments="69" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50063831" data-time="1791843329">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.sky.com/story/breaking-bad-star-rj-mitte-dies-aged-34-13598332" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>RJ Mitte has died</span><span class='zh'>RJ Mitte 去世</span></a><span class="dom">news.sky.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>RJ Mitte, the actor known for Breaking Bad, has died at 34.</span><span class='zh'>《绝命毒师》演员 RJ Mitte 去世，享年34岁。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>RJ Mitte, the actor known for Breaking Bad, has died at 34. His portrayal of Walter White Jr. helped humanize a central character, and his advocacy for autism awareness left an impact beyond the screen.</span><span class='zh'>《绝命毒师》演员 RJ Mitte 去世，享年34岁。他在剧中饰演的沃特·怀特 Jr. 让人物家庭一角更具人性，他还积极倡导自闭症意识，对影视界和公众的认知产生了影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://royfish.dev/fingerprints-are-the-new-cookies" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Fingerprints are the new cookies</span><span class='zh'>指纹识别成为新的网络追踪手段</span></a><span class="dom">royfish.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Browser fingerprinting persists as a tracking technique despite cookie erosion, enabling unique combos of device attributes to identify users across sites.</span><span class='zh'>尽管 cookies 逐渐被边缘化，浏览器指纹仍作为一种持续的跟踪手段，通过设备属性的组合实现跨站点识别。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Browser fingerprinting persists as a tracking technique despite cookie erosion, enabling unique combos of device attributes to identify users across sites. It underscores the ongoing tension between usability and privacy online.</span><span class='zh'>尽管 cookies 逐渐被边缘化，浏览器指纹仍作为一种持续的跟踪手段，通过设备属性的组合实现跨站点识别。文章讨论隐私影响及可能的对策，包括反指纹技术与浏览器保护措施，强调在线可用性与隐私之间的持续张力。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Data' style='--c:#6366f1'>Data</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>738</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50048642' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>360</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50057278" data-id="50057278" data-tags="Legal|Security|AI|Politics" data-score="329" data-comments="363" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50057278" data-time="1791816757">
<a class="thumb" href="https://mortoray.com/why-arent-ai-companies-being-charged-for-hacking/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#64748b' data-tag='Legal'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=mortoray.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>mortoray.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">32</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mortoray.com/why-arent-ai-companies-being-charged-for-hacking/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Why aren&#x27;t AI companies being charged for hacking?</span><span class='zh'>为什么 AI 公司没有因为黑客行为被起诉？</span></a><span class="dom">mortoray.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The post argues that charging AI companies for hacking is structurally difficult: attribution, intent, and the role of autonomous agents muddle liability, and current laws don’t map cleanly onto AI-enabled wrongdoing.</span><span class='zh'>文章指出，针对AI公司因黑客行为提起指控在法律上存在结构性困难：归责、意图与自动化代理的作用使责任难以界定，现行法难以直接套用到AI相关的违法行为。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The post argues that charging AI companies for hacking is structurally difficult: attribution, intent, and the role of autonomous agents muddle liability, and current laws don’t map cleanly onto AI-enabled wrongdoing. Until regulators clarify what counts as negligence or complicity, prosecutions against AI firms remain rare even when their tech is involved in breaches; the piece calls for clearer accountability frameworks to avoid chilling innovation.</span><span class='zh'>文章指出，针对AI公司因黑客行为提起指控在法律上存在结构性困难：归责、意图与自动化代理的作用使责任难以界定，现行法难以直接套用到AI相关的违法行为。除非监管框架明确企业在何种情形承担共犯或疏忽责任，否则相关起诉往往难以推进。作者呼吁建立更清晰的问责规则，以避免对创新造成过度约束。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Legal' style='--c:#64748b'>Legal</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>329</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50057278' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>363</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50054751" data-id="50054751" data-tags="Open Source|AI|Code" data-score="384" data-comments="407" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50054751" data-time="1791804144">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/qgis/QGIS-Enhancement-Proposals/pull/387" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#22c55e' data-tag='Open Source'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">33</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/qgis/QGIS-Enhancement-Proposals/pull/387" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>QGIS enacts moratorium on AI contributions</span><span class='zh'>QGIS 对 AI 贡献实施暂停</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>QGIS has enacted a moratorium on accepting AI-generated contributions while governance and licensing concerns are assessed.</span><span class='zh'>QGIS 已暂停接受基于 AI 的贡献，原因在于对许可证、质量和滥用风险的担忧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>QGIS has enacted a moratorium on accepting AI-generated contributions while governance and licensing concerns are assessed. The move signals open-source communities grappling with risk and quality control as AI tooling becomes more integrated. It may slow AI-assisted features in the GIS project but could set precedent for other OSS projects.</span><span class='zh'>QGIS 已暂停接受基于 AI 的贡献，原因在于对许可证、质量和滥用风险的担忧。此举显示开源社区在 AI 工具日益整合的环境下面临的风险与质量控制挑战，可能放慢该 GIS 项目中 AI 助力的功能部署，但也可能为其他开源项目树立先例。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>384</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50054751' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>407</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50061278" data-id="50061278" data-tags="Open Source|AI" data-score="231" data-comments="51" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50061278" data-time="1791832101">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://openfatigue.ai/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenFatigue™ – Open is the new closed</span><span class='zh'>OpenFatigue™——开放才是新封闭</span></a><span class="dom">openfatigue.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenFatigue presents an open-first stance with a collection of tools and policies designed to counteract vendor lock-in.</span><span class='zh'>OpenFatigue 提出以开放为先的做法，提供一套工具和政策以对抗厂商锁定。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenFatigue presents an open-first stance with a collection of tools and policies designed to counteract vendor lock-in. The project frames openness as a pathway to transparency, collaboration, and faster iteration in AI tooling and deployment.</span><span class='zh'>OpenFatigue 提出以开放为先的做法，提供一套工具和政策以对抗厂商锁定。该项目将开放视为提高透明度、协作与AI 工具迭代速度的途径。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Different communities—researchers, engineers, and users—hold divergent expectations about what LLMs can do.</span><span class='zh'>研究者、工程师和用户对 LLM 能力的认知正在拉开差距，易导致被炒作驱动的部署与政策不匹配。</span></p>

The piece advocates clearer benchmarks, transparent capability statements, and shared evaluation standards.</span><span class='zh'>研究者、工程师和用户对 LLM 能力的认知正在拉开差距，易导致被炒作驱动的部署与政策不匹配。文章主张建立更清晰的基准、公开的能力陈述和统一的评估标准。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://amosbbatto.wordpress.com/2026/10/05/linux-phones-alternative/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Will Linux phones become a viable alternative to the Android/iOS duopoly?</span><span class='zh'>Linux 手机会成为 Android/iOS 双头垄断的可行替代吗？</span></a><span class="dom">amosbbatto.wordpress.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece weighs hardware support, software ecosystems, and app availability as Linux phones mature.</span><span class='zh'>文章评估 Linux 手机在硬件支持、软件生态和应用生态等方面的成熟度。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece weighs hardware support, software ecosystems, and app availability as Linux phones mature. It highlights challenges like app parity and user experience but notes ongoing community efforts (e.g., postmarketOS, PinePhone) that could tip the balance if vendors and developers invest more.</span><span class='zh'>文章评估 Linux 手机在硬件支持、软件生态和应用生态等方面的成熟度。尽管存在应用生态不平衡和用户体验等挑战，但社区努力（如 postmarketOS、PinePhone）若获得更大投资，或许会逐步改变现状。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://theonion.com/archivehunt/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://theonion.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/onion-vol21-issue18-front-page.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='theonion.com' data-c='#d946ef' data-tag='Culture'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://theonion.com/archivehunt/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>So, you’d like to sell your print issues of The Onion?</span><span class='zh'>你愿意出售你的《The Onion》印刷版新闻吗？</span></a><span class="dom">theonion.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An exploration of selling vintage print issues of The Onion via an Archive Hunt-like platform, highlighting the quirks of media ephemera markets.</span><span class='zh'>探索通过类似 Archive Hunt 的平台出售《The Onion》的印刷旧刊，揭示媒体浮游文献市场的怪异现象。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An exploration of selling vintage print issues of The Onion via an Archive Hunt-like platform, highlighting the quirks of media ephemera markets. It touches on archiving culture, copyright questions, and the tension between physical artifacts and digital availability.</span><span class='zh'>探索通过类似 Archive Hunt 的平台出售《The Onion》的印刷旧刊，揭示媒体浮游文献市场的怪异现象。涉及档案文化、版权问题，以及实体与数字可用性之间的张力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://simonkoeck.com/writeups/google-devsite-flask-session-pickle-rce" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I Found a $90k Remote Code Execution Vulnerability in Google</span><span class='zh'>我在 Google 找到了价值 9 万美元的远程代码执行漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">simonkoeck.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Security researcher details a remote code execution vulnerability in Google&#x27;s DevSite Flask session handling via pickle deserialization, with a notable $90k bug bounty awarded.</span><span class='zh'>安全研究员披露了 Google DevSite 的 Flask 会话处理中通过 pickle 反序列化实现远程代码执行的漏洞，相关奖励达到约9万美元。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Security researcher details a remote code execution vulnerability in Google&#x27;s DevSite Flask session handling via pickle deserialization, with a notable $90k bug bounty awarded. The writeup highlights how unsafe deserialization can create RCE across web apps, and emphasizes the importance of strict input handling and patching.</span><span class='zh'>安全研究员披露了 Google DevSite 的 Flask 会话处理中通过 pickle 反序列化实现远程代码执行的漏洞，相关奖励达到约9万美元。该分析强调了反序列化漏洞的风险以及在 Web 应用中加强输入处理与及时修补的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://lalinsky.com/2026/10/11/type-safe-html-templating-in-zig.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Type-safe HTML templating in Zig</span><span class='zh'>在 Zig 中实现类型安全的 HTML 模板</span></a><span class="dom">lalinsky.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A type-safe HTML templating system is built in Zig using its compile-time features, providing strong guarantees against HTML injection and type errors.</span><span class='zh'>在 Zig 中实现了一套类型安全的 HTML 模板系统，利用编译时特性提供对 HTML 注入与类型错误的强保证。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A type-safe HTML templating system is built in Zig using its compile-time features, providing strong guarantees against HTML injection and type errors. The approach demonstrates how to implement templating that respects Zig&#x27;s safety while generating dynamic HTML, useful for embedded or systems projects that need UI.</span><span class='zh'>在 Zig 中实现了一套类型安全的 HTML 模板系统，利用编译时特性提供对 HTML 注入与类型错误的强保证。该方法展示如何在保持 Zig 安全性的同时生成动态 HTML，特别适合需要在嵌入式/系统项目中处理 UI 的场景。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Doory is a community-sourced, open-source design tool aiming to rival Canva, likely featuring templates, drag-and-drop editing, and export options.</span><span class='zh'>Doory 是一个开源的设计工具，旨在成为 Canva 的开源替代，具备模板、拖拽编辑和导出等功能。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Turns an unused Kindle into a smart photo frame powered by GPT-6 Astra, automatically curating and displaying images with contextual captions.</span><span class='zh'>将未使用的 Kindle 改造成搭载 GPT-6 Astra 的智能照片框，通过 AI 自动筛选并展示图片并附上情境说明。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Turns an unused Kindle into a smart photo frame powered by GPT-6 Astra, automatically curating and displaying images with contextual captions. The project demonstrates how AI can repurpose aging hardware, turning a simple device into a personalized, ambient display with contextual captions or suggestions. It&#x27;s a playful example of software extending hardware lifespans.</span><span class='zh'>将未使用的 Kindle 改造成搭载 GPT-6 Astra 的智能照片框，通过 AI 自动筛选并展示图片并附上情境说明。该项目展示了人工智能如何为老旧硬件赋予新生命，打造个性化的环境显示。是把软件能力延展到硬件寿命的新颖示例。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://doi.org/10.1111/acel.70751" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Blood cells from 80-year-old donor become stem cells with molecular age under 20</span><span class='zh'>80岁捐献者血细胞分子年龄重设为低于20岁水平</span></a><span class="dom">doi.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers converted blood cells from an 80-year-old donor into stem-cell like cells whose molecular aging markers register younger than 20.</span><span class='zh'>研究者将80岁捐献者的血细胞转化为干细胞样细胞，其分子年龄标记显示低于20岁。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers converted blood cells from an 80-year-old donor into stem-cell like cells whose molecular aging markers register younger than 20. The finding suggests cellular rejuvenation is possible in aged human cells, with potential for autologous regenerative therapies, though clinical translation remains distant.</span><span class='zh'>研究者将80岁捐献者的血细胞转化为干细胞样细胞，其分子年龄标记显示低于20岁。这表明对衰老细胞的年龄重置或许为个体化再生疗法提供方向，但临床落地仍需时日。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://jacobin.com/2026/10/books-publishing-reading-mass-market-paperback" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Publishers Killed the Paperback, Then Blamed Readers</span><span class='zh'>出版商杀死平装书？然后怪读者</span></a><span class="dom">jacobin.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A critical take on how publishers pushed away mass-market paperback formats in favor of digital or pricier editions, blaming readers for declines they partly caused.</span><span class='zh'>批评出版商推动平装市场式微，转而以数字版或高价版本获利，并把读者的阅读热情下降归咎于他们。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A critical take on how publishers pushed away mass-market paperback formats in favor of digital or pricier editions, blaming readers for declines they partly caused. The piece analyzes pricing, accessibility, and the business incentives shaping reading habits.</span><span class='zh'>批评出版商推动平装市场式微，转而以数字版或高价版本获利，并把读者的阅读热情下降归咎于他们。文章剖析定价、可及性与商业激励如何影响阅读习惯。</span></p>

The platform emphasizes isolation, reproducibility, and cost control, enabling teams to prototype prompts, policies, and multi-agent interactions.</span><span class='zh'>Agent37 提供低成本的沙盒环境，用以运行自主代理，降低了基于代理工作流的实验门槛。该平台强调隔离性、可重复性和成本控制，便于团队原型化提示、策略与多代理协作。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.githubstatus.com/incidents/93njd414rpbx" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Incident with Actions, Pull Requests and Issues</span><span class='zh'>Actions、Pull Requests 与 Issues 的事件</span></a><span class="dom">githubstatus.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GitHub reported an incident affecting Actions, Pull Requests, and Issues, causing degraded performance and delays across automation and project management features.</span><span class='zh'>GitHub 遭遇影响 Actions、Pull Requests 和 Issues 的事件，导致自动化与项目管理功能出现性能下降与延迟。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GitHub reported an incident affecting Actions, Pull Requests, and Issues, causing degraded performance and delays across automation and project management features. The incident page notes ongoing investigation with partial service restoration over time and guidance for users to retry operations and monitor status. This underscores how intertwined CI/CD in the platform is with everyday open-source workflows.</span><span class='zh'>GitHub 遭遇影响 Actions、Pull Requests 和 Issues 的事件，导致自动化与项目管理功能出现性能下降与延迟。事件页面持续更新调查进展，并给出重试与关注状态的指引。这再次凸显平台内置的CI/CD与日常开源工作流的高度耦合带来的脆弱性。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://clickhouse.com/blog/posette-talk-recap-postgres-isnt-slow-your-storage-is" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://clickhouse.com/_next/image?url=%2Fuploads%2FPostgres_Isn_t_Slow_Your_Storage_Is_8e9d00bdc5.jpg&amp;w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;q=75' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='clickhouse.com' data-c='#6366f1' data-tag='Data'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://clickhouse.com/blog/posette-talk-recap-postgres-isnt-slow-your-storage-is" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Postgres isn&#x27;t slow, your storage is</span><span class='zh'>Postgres 不慢，存储才是瓶颈</span></a><span class="dom">clickhouse.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>PostgreSQL performance is often limited by storage I/O rather than the database engine itself.</span><span class='zh'>PostgreSQL 的性能经常被存储 I/O 所拖累，而非数据库本身。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PostgreSQL performance is often limited by storage I/O rather than the database engine itself. Optimizations should focus on storage subsystems—fast disks, SSDs, caching, and WAL throughput—to unlock real gains, with SQL tuning being secondary to I/O considerations.</span><span class='zh'>PostgreSQL 的性能经常被存储 I/O 所拖累，而非数据库本身。真正的提升往往来自优化存储子系统，如更快的磁盘、SSD、缓存和 WAL 吞吐量，而非单纯调优 SQL。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Data' style='--c:#6366f1'>Data</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>150</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50052163' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>162</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50055409" data-id="50055409" data-tags="Security|Science" data-score="87" data-comments="29" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50055409" data-time="1791807992">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://words.filippo.io/right-answers/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>(Post-Quantum) Cryptographic Right Answers</span><span class='zh'>后量子密码学的正确答案</span></a><span class="dom">words.filippo.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Offers pragmatic guidance on post-quantum cryptography, including algorithm selection, migration paths, and hybrid approaches to maintain security during the transition.</span><span class='zh'>就后量子密码学提供务实指引，涵盖算法选择、迁移路径以及混合方案，以在过渡期间维持安全性。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author paid participants to try following his README, turning documentation into a live test. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The post frames Ruby&#x27;s current era as a &#x27;Weimar&#x27; period—creative energy amid political and organizational volatility, with debates over governance, funding, and direction.</span><span class='zh'>将 Ruby 的当前生态比作魏玛时代，充满变动、分叉与关于语言走向与治理的激烈辩论。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The post frames Ruby&#x27;s current era as a &#x27;Weimar&#x27; period—creative energy amid political and organizational volatility, with debates over governance, funding, and direction. It analyzes how the ecosystem navigates drama, forks, and evolving language features, shaping its long-term resilience.</span><span class='zh'>将 Ruby 的当前生态比作魏玛时代，充满变动、分叉与关于语言走向与治理的激烈辩论。文章讨论社区、维护者和企业支持如何共同塑造语言的短期走向与韧性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ircv3.net/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>IRCv3</span><span class='zh'>IRCv3 规范</span></a><span class="dom">ircv3.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>IRCv3 defines modern extensions to the IRC protocol, enabling capability negotiation and richer metadata between clients and servers.</span><span class='zh'>IRCv3 定义了对 IRC 协议的现代化扩展，支持客户端与服务器之间的能力协商与更丰富的元数据。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>IRCv3 defines modern extensions to the IRC protocol, enabling capability negotiation and richer metadata between clients and servers. It aims to make IRC more usable and interoperable in a world of feature-rich clients. The spec underpins improvements like extended joins and better identity handling.</span><span class='zh'>IRCv3 定义了对 IRC 协议的现代化扩展，支持客户端与服务器之间的能力协商与更丰富的元数据。它旨在让 IRC 在功能丰富的客户端时代更易用、互操作性更强。该规范支撑了扩展加入、身份处理等改进。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A retrospective look at holography as of 2011, tracing its promise in 3D imaging, data storage, and display against the realities of materials and manufacturing costs.</span><span class='zh'>回顾截至2011年的全息术发展，聚焦于3D成像、数据存储与显示的潜力与现实之间的矛盾。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A retrospective look at holography as of 2011, tracing its promise in 3D imaging, data storage, and display against the realities of materials and manufacturing costs. It explains why practical, widespread holo-imaging remains elusive despite decades of research.</span><span class='zh'>回顾截至2011年的全息术发展，聚焦于3D成像、数据存储与显示的潜力与现实之间的矛盾。尽管研究持续，实际大规模应用仍受材料、制造成本与可扩展性等瓶颈制约。文章提醒读者，成像质量、规模化和成本是关键挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://tedhaley.ca/pensieve/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Local Indexing to reduce tool calling and token usage</span><span class='zh'>本地索引以减少工具调用和令牌消耗</span></a><span class="dom">tedhaley.ca</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Introduces local indexing to cut down external tool invocations and token usage in AI workflows, improving speed and reducing costs.</span><span class='zh'>提出通过本地索引来减少外部工具调用和 tokens 使用，在 AI 工作流中提升速度并降低成本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Introduces local indexing to cut down external tool invocations and token usage in AI workflows, improving speed and reducing costs. The approach relies on caching or embedding domain-specific knowledge locally so agents can answer without round-trips.</span><span class='zh'>提出通过本地索引来减少外部工具调用和 tokens 使用，在 AI 工作流中提升速度并降低成本。这种方法借助本地缓存或领域知识的嵌入，使代理在本地就能给出答案，减少远程请求。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://pub.sakana.ai/mass/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Recursive self-improvement through collective intelligence</span><span class='zh'>通过集体智慧实现的递归自我提升</span></a><span class="dom">pub.sakana.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Examines how collective intelligence could fuel recursive self-improvement in AI systems, via feedback and collaboration among humans and agents.</span><span class='zh'>研究通过集体智慧驱动 AI 的递归自我提升的可能性，借助人机与多代理的协作与反馈形成自我改进循环。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Examines how collective intelligence could fuel recursive self-improvement in AI systems, via feedback and collaboration among humans and agents. Iterative loops could push capabilities beyond a single-model ceiling, but governance and alignment become more complex at scale.</span><span class='zh'>研究通过集体智慧驱动 AI 的递归自我提升的可能性，借助人机与多代理的协作与反馈形成自我改进循环。随着协同放大能力，进展可能超出单一模型的上限，但规模化时在治理和对齐方面的挑战也随之增加。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.ways-of-seeing.com/ch1" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Chapter 1 of &quot;Ways of Seeing&quot; by John Berger (1972)</span><span class='zh'>约翰·伯杰《观看之道》第一章（1972）</span></a><span class="dom">ways-of-seeing.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Chapter 1 dissects how seeing is shaped by social constructs, power, and media, challenging conventional perceptions of art.</span><span class='zh'>第一章批判性揭示了看到如何被社会结构、权力与媒介所塑造，挑战了对艺术的直观理解。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Chapter 1 dissects how seeing is shaped by social constructs, power, and media, challenging conventional perceptions of art. It lays groundwork for media literacy and critical interpretation of images—relevant to tech culture&#x27;s visuals and AI-generated imagery.</span><span class='zh'>第一章批判性揭示了看到如何被社会结构、权力与媒介所塑造，挑战了对艺术的直观理解。为媒介素养和对图像的批判性解读奠定基础，与科技文化中的视觉表达和 AI 生成图像密切相关。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.sciencealert.com/secret-of-orange-cats-finally-revealed-after-60-year-search" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Secret of Orange Cats Revealed After 60-Year Search</span><span class='zh'>橙色猫的秘密在60年的追寻后揭示</span></a><span class="dom">sciencealert.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A long-running genetic mystery about orange cats has finally been solved: orange fur is linked to a pigment gene on the X chromosome, meaning most orange cats are male and female orange cats are rarities caused by X-inactivation or unusual genetic events.</span><span class='zh'>关于橙色猫的长期遗传谜团终被揭晓：橙色毛色由位于X染色体上的色素基因控制，因此大多为公猫，雌性橙猫罕见，通常源于X染色体失活或异常基因事件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A long-running genetic mystery about orange cats has finally been solved: orange fur is linked to a pigment gene on the X chromosome, meaning most orange cats are male and female orange cats are rarities caused by X-inactivation or unusual genetic events. The finding clarifies how simple color genetics interact with sex chromosome biology and explains the observed distribution of orange cats in populations.</span><span class='zh'>关于橙色猫的长期遗传谜团终被揭晓：橙色毛色由位于X染色体上的色素基因控制，因此大多为公猫，雌性橙猫罕见，通常源于X染色体失活或异常基因事件。研究揭示了颜色遗传如何与性染色体生物学相互作用，以及橙猫在人群中的分布特征。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://datacenters.google/locations/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google Data Centers around the world</span><span class='zh'>谷歌全球数据中心分布</span></a><span class="dom">datacenters.google</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google operates a vast, world-spanning data-center network to support search, cloud services, and AI workloads, with attention to latency, redundancy, and energy sourcing.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌运营着覆盖全球的数据中心网络，以支撑搜索、云服务和 AI 工作负载，强调低时延、冗余与能源采购。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google operates a vast, world-spanning data-center network to support search, cloud services, and AI workloads, with attention to latency, redundancy, and energy sourcing. The data-centre footprint underpins modern internet services.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌运营着覆盖全球的数据中心网络，以支撑搜索、云服务和 AI 工作负载，强调低时延、冗余与能源采购。文章点出规模、区域性考量及可持续承诺和监管影响，强调数据中心是现代互联网服务的基础设施。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.gq-magazine.co.uk/article/a-decade-after-stardew-valley-eric-barone-is-still-going-it-alone" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A decade after Stardew Valley, Eric Barone is still going it alone</span><span class='zh'>Stardew Valley 十年后，Eric Barone 仍独自一人继续开发</span></a><span class="dom">gq-magazine.co.uk</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ten years after Stardew Valley, Eric Barone continues to develop solo, expanding the game&#x27;s universe and modding ecosystem while managing indie studio constraints.</span><span class='zh'>在《星露谷物语》上市十年后，Eric Barone 仍然以个人方式推动开发，扩展游戏世界和模组生态，同时应对独立工作室的资源限制。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ten years after Stardew Valley, Eric Barone continues to develop solo, expanding the game&#x27;s universe and modding ecosystem while managing indie studio constraints.</span><span class='zh'>在《星露谷物语》上市十年后，Eric Barone 仍然以个人方式推动开发，扩展游戏世界和模组生态，同时应对独立工作室的资源限制。文章强调个人开发的持久魅力、资源有限的取舍，以及一个创作者如何维持长期项目的热度与持续性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://pixelatron.com/blog/the-making-of-super-mario-64-full-giles-goddard-interview-ngc/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The Making Of Super Mario 64 – full Giles Goddard interview (2001)</span><span class='zh'>《超级马里奥64》制作内幕完整访谈（Giles Goddard，2001）</span></a><span class="dom">pixelatron.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This full 2001 interview with Giles Goddard reveals how Super Mario 64’s groundbreaking 3D movement and camera system were conceived, including the challenges of porting to Nintendo 64 hardware and the iterative design that bridged 2D Mario sensibilities into 3D space.</span><span class='zh'>这段2001年的完整访谈揭示了《超级马里奥64》在3D移动与摄像机系统方面的开创性构思，以及在任天堂64硬件上的移植与实现所面临的挑战。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This full 2001 interview with Giles Goddard reveals how Super Mario 64’s groundbreaking 3D movement and camera system were conceived, including the challenges of porting to Nintendo 64 hardware and the iterative design that bridged 2D Mario sensibilities into 3D space. It highlights the craft of early 3D game development and the engineering trade-offs that shaped a milestone in the platformer genre.</span><span class='zh'>这段2001年的完整访谈揭示了《超级马里奥64》在3D移动与摄像机系统方面的开创性构思，以及在任天堂64硬件上的移植与实现所面临的挑战。通过对早期3D游戏开发过程的讲解，展现了将2D马里奥直觉转化为3D空间所需的设计与工程权衡，及其为何成为平台跳跃游戏的里程碑。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://vcresearch.berkeley.edu/news/using-ai-just-10-minutes-erodes-your-ability-persist-hard-things" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Using AI for just 10 minutes erodes your ability to persist at hard things</span><span class='zh'>仅用 AI 十分钟就会削弱你坚持困难任务的能力</span></a><span class="dom">vcresearch.berkeley.edu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Berkeley study finds that even brief, 10-minute AI usage can erode persistence on hard tasks, prompting questions about cognitive effects of AI assistance.</span><span class='zh'>伯克利研究发现，即使仅使用 AI 十分钟也会削弱坚持艰难任务的能力，这对教育、生产力以及在 AI 时代如何设计任务具有重要意义。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Berkeley study finds that even brief, 10-minute AI usage can erode persistence on hard tasks, prompting questions about cognitive effects of AI assistance. The finding has implications for education, productivity, and how tasks are structured in the age of AI.</span><span class='zh'>伯克利研究发现，即使仅使用 AI 十分钟也会削弱坚持艰难任务的能力，这对教育、生产力以及在 AI 时代如何设计任务具有重要意义。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rogerbinns/apsw" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>apsw: Another Python SQLite Wrapper</span><span class='zh'>apsw：又一个 Python SQLite 封装器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>APSW offers a Python SQLite wrapper with lower-level access and extra features beyond the standard interface.</span><span class='zh'>APSW 是一个为 Python 提供 SQLite 封装的库，提供比内置 sqlite3 更低层次的访问和扩展功能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>APSW offers a Python SQLite wrapper with lower-level access and extra features beyond the standard interface. It exposes advanced APIs for virtual tables, custom collations, and direct blob handling, appealing to power users who need fine-grained control—though it requires more maintenance.</span><span class='zh'>APSW 是一个为 Python 提供 SQLite 封装的库，提供比内置 sqlite3 更低层次的访问和扩展功能。它支持高级 API，如虚拟表、定制排序以及直接操作 BLOB，适合需要更细粒度控制的高级开发者；不过使用起来需要更多手工维护和编码约束。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Geramy/LemonSeed-Studio" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Running the unmodified Linux amdgpu driver on an iPad</span><span class='zh'>在 iPad 上运行未修改的 Linux amdgpu 驱动</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An experimental project demonstrates loading the unmodified amdgpu driver on iPad hardware, highlighting the gaps between Linux driver support and consumer devices.</span><span class='zh'>一项实验性尝试在 iPad 硬件上运行未修改的 amdgpu 驱动，揭示了 Linux 驱动与消费级设备之间的差距。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An experimental project demonstrates loading the unmodified amdgpu driver on iPad hardware, highlighting the gaps between Linux driver support and consumer devices. It underlines the significant hurdles in achieving functional GPU acceleration and full system usability on non-traditional hardware.</span><span class='zh'>一项实验性尝试在 iPad 硬件上运行未修改的 amdgpu 驱动，揭示了 Linux 驱动与消费级设备之间的差距。强调在非传统硬件上实现显卡加速与完整系统可用性所面临的重大挑战。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A 2024 exploration of how a single cell behaves with remarkable autonomy and information processing, exemplified by E.</span><span class='zh'>这篇 2024 年的文章探讨单细胞在没有神经系统的情况下如何展现出近乎智能的行为，以大肠杆菌趋化为例，揭示简单生物也具备复杂的信息处理能力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://phys.org/news/2024-02-scandinavia-farmers-slaughtered-hunter-population.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Scandinavia&#x27;s first farmers slaughtered hunter-gatherer population, DNA suggests</span><span class='zh'>DNA 显示斯堪的纳维亚首批农民屠杀猎人-采集者</span></a><span class="dom">phys.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ancient DNA suggests early farming populations replaced or violently displaced hunter-gatherers in Scandinavia, shedding light on population dynamics during Europe&#x27;s transition to agriculture.</span><span class='zh'>古代DNA研究显示，斯堪的纳维亚早期农民群体在向农耕社会过渡时，替代或暴力驱逐了猎人-采集者群体，为欧洲史前期人口动态的讨论提供新证据，涉及迁徙、文化与冲突。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ancient DNA suggests early farming populations replaced or violently displaced hunter-gatherers in Scandinavia, shedding light on population dynamics during Europe&#x27;s transition to agriculture. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://sebsite.pw/w/20261009-strtod.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>No portable way to detect string-to-float conversion errors in standard C</span><span class='zh'>标准 C 中没有可移植的字符串转浮点数错误检测方法</span></a><span class="dom">sebsite.pw</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Standard C offers no portable, uniform signal when strtod fails or overflows, making robust error handling difficult across platforms.</span><span class='zh'>标准 C 未提供可移植、统一的字符串转浮点数错误信号，跨平台的错误处理变得困难。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Standard C offers no portable, uniform signal when strtod fails or overflows, making robust error handling difficult across platforms. The post likely outlines practical patterns or workarounds to reliably detect conversion errors, suggesting safer parsing strategies for C libraries.</span><span class='zh'>标准 C 未提供可移植、统一的字符串转浮点数错误信号，跨平台的错误处理变得困难。文中给出若干可行的模式或变通方案，以更可靠地检测转换错误，并提出更安全的解析策略。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Bag of Decisions reranker presents a framework to improve decision-making in models by ranking candidate decisions using a diverse feature set, aiming to outperform single-step generation.</span><span class='zh'>‘Bag of Decisions’ 重排序器提出一个通过多特征对候选决策排序的框架，旨在优于单步生成。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Bag of Decisions reranker presents a framework to improve decision-making in models by ranking candidate decisions using a diverse feature set, aiming to outperform single-step generation. The post outlines how to combine multiple signals to select better outcomes, with practical guidance for implementing rerankers in ML pipelines.</span><span class='zh'>‘Bag of Decisions’ 重排序器提出一个通过多特征对候选决策排序的框架，旨在优于单步生成。文章阐述了如何将多种信号结合以选出更优结果，并给出在机器学习管线中实现重排序器的实用指南。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Explains how to implement clamp-leading-zeros and count-trailing-zeros using vector instructions (SIMD), enabling faster bit-scan operations in performance-critical code.</span><span class='zh'>讲解如何使用向量化指令（SIMD）实现 CLZ 与 CTZ，从而在对性能敏感的代码中加速位扫描操作。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Explores how to use SIMD in Rust with safety guarantees, addressing unsafe blocks and presenting safer abstractions or crates that expose SIMD benefits without compromising memory safety.</span><span class='zh'>探讨在 Rust 中实现带有安全保障的 SIMD，解决不安全代码的问题，并介绍更安全的抽象或库以在不牺牲内存安全的情况下获得 SIMD 性能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Explores how to use SIMD in Rust with safety guarantees, addressing unsafe blocks and presenting safer abstractions or crates that expose SIMD benefits without compromising memory safety. Highlights performance wins for numeric workloads and implications for libraries.</span><span class='zh'>探讨在 Rust 中实现带有安全保障的 SIMD，解决不安全代码的问题，并介绍更安全的抽象或库以在不牺牲内存安全的情况下获得 SIMD 性能。强调对数值工作负载的性能收益以及对库的影响。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">114</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://medium.com/@timventura/akhetonics-photonic-computing-1-000x-faster-cpus-b9e91c470079" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Akhetonics Photonic Computing: 1,000x Faster CPUs</span><span class='zh'>Akhetonics 光子计算：CPU 提升一千倍</span></a><span class="dom">medium.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Akhetonics claims photonic computing could deliver up to 1,000x speedups for CPUs, driven by light-based processing.</span><span class='zh'>报道称 Akhetonics 的光子计算有望让 CPU 达到高达 1000x 的速度提升，依赖光信号处理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Akhetonics claims photonic computing could deliver up to 1,000x speedups for CPUs, driven by light-based processing. While the prospect is tantalizing for AI workloads and energy efficiency, real-world practicality faces material, fabrication, and integration hurdles before it becomes practical.</span><span class='zh'>报道称 Akhetonics 的光子计算有望让 CPU 达到高达 1000x 的速度提升，依赖光信号处理。对 AI 工作负载和能效潜在影响重大，但在材料、制造与整合方面仍存挑战。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.nytimes.com/2026/10/11/opinion/clutter-spring-cleaning-psychology.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#4ade80' data-tag='Health'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=nytimes.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>nytimes.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">115</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nytimes.com/2026/10/11/opinion/clutter-spring-cleaning-psychology.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Neuroscience can make decluttering easier</span><span class='zh'>神经科学能让收纳整理变得更容易</span></a><span class="dom">nytimes.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Neuroscience-informed strategies claim to make decluttering easier by aligning habits with how the brain rewards attention.</span><span class='zh'>神经科学观点认为，通过对齐习惯与大脑奖励机制和注意力运作，可以让整理杂物变得更容易执行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Neuroscience-informed strategies claim to make decluttering easier by aligning habits with how the brain rewards attention. Practical tips include reducing friction in the environment and building micro-habits that reinforce progress. The piece reframes tidying as a cognitive-behavioral task rather than a purely motivational one.</span><span class='zh'>神经科学观点认为，通过对齐习惯与大脑奖励机制和注意力运作，可以让整理杂物变得更容易执行。提出的实用建议包括降低整理时的阻力、建立能巩固进步的微习惯等。文章将整理从单纯的动机问题转化为认知行为层面的任务。</span></p>
